美時 (1795 TT)  Trading Buy

2026 年毛利率回升，海外 ECB 再融資計畫落地將降低利
息負擔。  
投資建議 ：(1) Alvogen 併購效益與全球 B2B 業務，產品組合優
化趨勢確立。營收規模翻倍且產品組合改善帶動毛利率回升；
(2) 高壁壘產品線進入密集收割期 ， 重磅 505(b)(2) Cabozantinib
預計 4Q26 審查完成。 (3) 預估 2026 EPS 12.08 元，投資建議
Trading Buy ，目標價 200 元。  
2Q26 在併購營收規模擴張下，毛利率與獲利修復動能，但淨利
仍受到併購融資之高額財務利息與一次性稅負壓抑：  
2Q26 營收 88.09 億，毛利率為 53.68% ，較 1Q26 的 50.73% 提
升，主要反映高毛利產品出貨回升與產品組合轉佳。費用顯著
攀升主要係合併 Alvogen 後營運規模擴張所致。  
營業外收支部分， 1H26 非營業費用 25.76 億，其中財務成本達
23.67 億 (YoY+980.82%) ，主要受到美國較高利率環境下產生龐
大財務利息成本，以及期間美元升值產生之外幣匯兌損失衝擊。
所得稅方面， 1H26 認列非經常性一次性稅務項目，若排除該項
一次性影響，調整後有效稅率為 22.3% 。  
2H26 美時將以 ECB 再融資顯著減輕利息成本為獲利修復核心 ：  
受惠於併購 Alvogen 後的完整年度營收挹注 、 7 月起併入 Sandoz
菲律賓業務、韓國 Uro-Vaxom 經銷啟動，以及 Nintedanib 與
Enzalutamide 等 B2B 產品持續在全球多國鋪貨出貨， 2026 全年
營收將維持翻倍增長曲線。隨著 2Q26 產品組合優化使毛利率
回升至 53.7% ，預期 2H26 在高毛利癌症藥物與自製困難學名藥
出貨比例提升下，全年毛利率將維持在 52%–54% 區間。  
美時海外可轉換公司債 (ECB) 發行案已正式獲得證期局核准，將
積極推進債務再融資計畫，目標是大幅置換現有高利率貸款、
顯著降低下半年及未來的財務利息費用，進一步釋放獲利彈性
並提升資金調度靈活度。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 2 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
美時之企業使命「提供全球病患更好、更安全且更負擔得起的藥物」。為追
求使命，同時投入環境與社會之永續發展， 2024 年初，美時已成立「永續
暨風險管理小組」 ，針對公司的永續發展工作建立管理機制，研擬永續發展
相關之短、中、長期目標、策略，並管理監督跨部門之執行過程與成果，以
從系統性的內部管理，極小化美時產品對於社會及環境所帶來的潛在衝擊。
公司簡介：  
美時專精於高門檻的複雜學名藥及癌症用藥的研發、生產與銷售。公司產品
線涵蓋中樞神經系統、癌症、心血管疾病等領域，並在美國、歐洲及亞洲多
國市場擁有廣泛的銷售通路。  
透過策略性併購與全球市場的布局，美時已逐步從區域性藥廠轉型為具備
全球影響力的國際製藥公司。  
2Q26 在併購營收規模擴張下，毛利率與獲利修復動能，但淨利仍受到併購
融資之高額財務利息與一次性稅負壓抑：  
2Q26 營收 88.09 億， 1H26 累計營收 174.53 億 (YoY+84.18%) ，創下歷史半
年度新高紀錄。 2Q26 毛利率為 53.68% ，較 1Q26 的 50.73% 提升 3 個百分
點，主要反映高毛利產品出貨回升與產品組合轉佳。 1H26 營業費用合計
48.22 億 (YoY+93.65%) ，其中管銷費用為 42.96 億 (YoY+97.06%) 、研發費用
為 5.26 億 (YoY+69.68%) ，費用顯著攀升主要係合併 Alvogen 後營運規模擴
張所致。  
營業外收支部分 ，1H26 非營業費用合計高達 25.76 億 ， 其中財務成本達23.67
億 (YoY+980.82%) ，其他營業外淨支出為 2.09 億 (YoY-51.40%) ，主要受到美
國較高利率環境下產生龐大財務利息成本，以及期間美元升值產生之外幣
匯兌損失衝擊。所得稅方面， 1H26 認列非經常性一次性稅務項目，使本期
實際有效稅率暫時提升至 29.5% ；若排除該項一次性影響，調整後有效稅率
為 22.3% 。  
區域營收佔比與年增：  
市場區域 1H26 佔
比 1H26 金額變動(YoY) 變動說明 
美國 62% +68.97 億(+180%YoY) 併購 Alvogen 業務 
韓國 17% -0.27 億(微幅下滑) 既有成熟產品線自然波動 
B2B 業務 8% +10.42 億(+245%YoY) Nintedanib及 Enzalutamide全
球授權夥伴拉貨爆發 
台灣 7% -0.35 億(微幅下滑) 健保藥價定期調整 
東南亞 5% +0.78 億 區域通路擴展及產品送件核
准放量 
亞洲其他 1% +0.21 億 新興市場授權合作 
1H26 營收在地理分佈上呈現重大轉變，美國市場在 Alvogen 完整合併後營
收大幅增長 180% 達 68.97 億，營收佔比攀升至 62% ，成為公司最核心的營
運市場； B2B 全球外銷業務表現極為亮眼，受惠於 Nintedanib 於歐洲 27 國、
加拿大、台灣與美國上市出貨，以及 Enzalutamide 於加拿大、巴西、以色列

3 群益投顧 
與韓國上市放量， B2B 營收暴增 245% 達 10.42 億，佔比升至 8%；亞洲既有
市場整體保持平穩 (+1%YoY) ，其中東南亞增加 0.78 億、亞洲其他增加 0.21
億，有效抵銷韓國 (-0.27 億 )與台灣 (-0.35 億 )的微幅回檔。  
2H26 美時將以 ECB 再融資顯著減輕利息成本為獲利修復核心：  
受惠於併購 Alvogen 後的完整年度營收挹注、 7 月起併入 Sandoz 菲律賓業
務、韓國 Uro-Vaxom 經銷啟動，以及 Nintedanib 與 Enzalutamide 等 B2B 產
品持續在全球多國鋪貨出貨， 2026 全年營收將維持翻倍增長曲線。在毛利
率表現上，隨著 2Q26 產品組合優化使毛利率回升至 53.7% ，預期 2H26 在
高毛利癌症藥物與自製困難學名藥出貨比例提升下，全年毛利率將維持在
52%–54% 區間。  
美時海外可轉換公司債 (ECB) 發行案已正式獲得證期局核准，將積極推進債
務再融資計畫，目標是大幅置換現有高利率貸款、顯著降低下半年及未來的
財務利息費用，進一步釋放獲利彈性並提升資金調度靈活度。  
中長期來看，管線中超過三分之一的專案將於 2026 至 2028 年密集推出，產
品上市能見度極高，將推動公司在 2026 年之後維持成長動能。  
投資建議 ：(1) Alvogen 併購效益與全球 B2B 業務，產品組合優化趨勢確立。
營收規模翻倍且產品組合改善帶動毛利率回升； (2) 高壁壘產品線進入密集
收割期，重磅 505(b)(2) Cabozantinib 預計 4Q26 審查完成。 (3) 預估 2026 
EPS 12.08 元，投資建議 Trading Buy ，目標價 200 元。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

豐興(2015) 
區間操作, TP 68 (潛在報酬 6.58%)  
區間操作 
Price(08/31)：63.8 
Target Price：68

豐興 26Q2 雖營收年減 9.20% 至 75.87 億元，但受惠型鋼與條
線出貨成長及產品組合優化，毛利率攀升至 12.74%；加計業外認
列鋼聯與豐堉資源之貢獻 ， 稅後淨利達 8.47 億元 ，YoY 26.61%，
EPS 為 1.45 元。  展望 2026 全年，受鋼筋出貨承壓影響，預
估營收為 272.14 億元 ，YoY -6.28%； 但在型鋼與條線出貨維穩，
以及第二季業外收益的挹注下 ， 預估全年稅後淨利將成長至 25.31 
億元，YoY 12.28%，預估全年 EPS 為 4.35 元。   
評價方面 ， 按照過往 75% 配息率估算 ，2027 年有望配發 3.2 元
現金股利， 隱含殖利率約 5.1%。 考量獲利雖優於 2025 年且具備
殖利率支撐 ， 但未來整體營運動能受限 ， 故給予歷史本淨比中緣位
階 1.7 倍 PBR 評價，目標價設定為 68 元，投資建議為區間操
作。 
⚫   ⚫ 公司基本資料 
豐興鋼鐵(2015)成立於 1969 年，為台灣主要電爐鋼廠之一，
也是國內唯一同時具備型鋼 、 條線及鋼筋三大產品線的煉軋合
一鋼鐵廠。 
產品組合方面，鋼筋約佔營收 54%，市占率約 14.4%、條線
佔 25%、型鋼佔 21%。產能配置方面，豐興擁有85 噸 DC 電
弧爐和 100 噸 AC 電弧爐各一座 ， 搭配三條直列式軋鋼設備，
年產能達 180 萬噸。公司採用垂直整合的生產模式，具備完
整的煉鋼到軋鋼生產線，不僅可自給自足鋼胚，更能有效控制
生產成本。在原料採購策略上，豐興採取分批小量採購的方
式，有效降低原料價格波動風險。主要原料廢鐵和鋼胚約占成
本的六到七成，

⚫ 鋼筋銷售下滑，營收年減 9.20%至 75.87 億，EPS 1.45 元 
26Q2 鋼筋銷售年減 2 成，型鋼出貨與條線出貨年增，合併營
收 75.87 億，QoQ 18.47% ，YoY -9.20%；獲利受出貨季增
且產品組合優化 ， 毛利率升至 12.74%， 季增 1.20 個百分點，
年增 1.33 個百分點；營業利益 7.34 億，QoQ 24.47%，YoY 
-0.90%；業外認列鋼聯與豐堉資源之貢獻，稅後獲利攀升至  
8.47 億，QoQ 60.83%，YoY 26.61%；EPS 為 1.45 元。

型鋼需求表現堅挺，預期全年出貨呈現持平至小幅成長，條線
目前雖處庫存消化階段，且下游需求呈分化 （螺帽與機械零組
件相對穩健，螺絲與手工具偏保守） ，但兩項產品展現出強韌
的定價能力；在原料跌價時報價依然穩定，價格與利差具備實
質支撐，預期將成為後市營收與獲利的核心動能。   
綜合評估，在鋼筋出貨承壓、型鋼與條線獲利護航的結構下，
預估全年營收為 272.14 億元，YoY -6.28%。受惠於產品組
合優化，預估毛利率將提升至  12.71%，年增 1.18 個百分
點，營業利益達 25.91 億元，YoY 3.54% 。加計第二季轉投
資等業外收益的顯著挹注，預估稅後淨利將攀升至 25.31 億
元，YoY 12.28%， 全年 EPS 為 4.35 元 ， 每股淨值約 39.91 
元。

### Page 1

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co.

SEMICON Taiwan: proliferating AI boosting 
the manufacturing innovation

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 3,100 TWD | Price: 2,420 TWD

HPC computing drives holistic manufacturing innovation 
SEMICON Taiwan kicked off on August 31st with IC Forum and Silicon Photonics Summit, 
both featuring TSMC’s presentations. At the session in IC Forum, TSMC shared its view 
that the data center power capacity will accelerate from 5-6GW per year from ‘15-24 to 
30-40GW per year from ‘25-30, reaching 300GW installed base by ’30 as AI training 
scales toward multimodal (5x per annum LLM compute growth). In addition, it sees 
robust AI inference demand, with token consumption growing ~500x since ’22 led by 
agents and reasoning and further compounding by 24x to 120 quadrillion tokens by ‘30. 
TSMC has AI platform across logic (A13, A12, N2U). Si-technology for HBM scaling (from 
N12 for HBM4 to N3 for HBM5) and system integration (e.g. SoIC with 11 tape outs for 
GPU/CPU by 4Q26 and >14x reticle size CoWoS by ‘29) and compute fabric (e.g. CPO). 
Silicon photonics acceleration to enable faster I/O scaling 
TSMC believes the optical I/O is becoming prerequisite for AI/HPC system as the I/O gap 
is widening between the compute scaling (3x every 2 years) and I/O scaling (1.4x every 
two years). Its COUPE platform and a broad photonics device/PDK library facilitate its 
customer designs to enhance the connectivity through two complementary paths – 
“faster and narrowing” with higher per lane speed (200Gbps to 400Gbps) and lane count 
(128+ lanes) and “slower and wider” by increasing wavelengths from 1 to 16+ through 
WDM. To allow further production ramp and wider adoption of the technology platform, 
TSMC has lowered COUPE grating coupler insertion loss while improved edge coupler 
performance and sees wafer level testing is critical to reduce the wasted packaging cost. 
Stock at mid-cycle supported by robust AI demand pipeline 
We maintain our Buy rating with PO at NT$3,100 on 20x '27 P/E. The share valuation is 
undemanding at 16x '27 P/E on strong technology leadership and extending AI visibility.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
TSMC was founded in 1987 and is listed in Taiwan 
(2330TT) and the US (TSM/NYS).TSMC is the largest and 
global leader in integrated circuit (IC) manufacturing. As a 
build-to-order foundry, it provides a wide range of value-
add activities: IC manufacturing, mask-making, IC design 
services, turnkey solutions, and process development. We 
attribute its success to its proven, winning business model, 
unparalleled scale advantage, optimized execution, and 
technology scope and depth. 5 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
The ongoing semi content growth in mobile, rise of artificial 
intelligence (AI), and proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) 
should result in sustainable upside in aggregate computing 
power globally. TSMC, as the leading contract manufacturer 
of semiconductor chips, is in a good position to capitalize. 
We are structurally positive on TSMC and expect it to 
sustain over 15% growth with rising FCF from 2026 
onwards.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMWF / TSM) 
Our PO for TSMC is NT$3,100 per share (US$590 per ADR), based on 20x 2027E P/E. 
TSMC's valuation has traded in the range of 10-26x PE from 2017. 20x is in the mid to 
high end of the historical range, above the average P/E of 18x. We think a PE above the 
average is justified given TSMC's enhanced industry position and mid-40% 5-year CAGR 
in AI should underpin a rerating

Downside risks to our price objective are: (1) greater-than-expected slowdown in global 
smartphone/consumer electronics demand, (2) Intel's potential insourcing strategy and 
ambitions in foundry service, (3) execution risks on advanced nodes, and (4) higher than 
expected tariffs.

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-31

### Page 1

Summary: We hosted an online investor conference call for Kinsus after trading hours on 
31 August. We believe that management’s current revenue target is 15% QoQ growth in 
3Q26 (vs. market expectation of 10% QoQ). Regarding the investigation into Unimicron’s 
original place of production, the company noted that it has not seen any order shift from 
end customers. However, it believes this event will not be a negative factor for other 
substrate makers. In addition, Kinsus’ 9% share price decline today has nothing to do with 
the investigation into Unimicron. Rather, it is more likely related to its parent company’s 
plan to sell 16 million shares in Kinsus (announced last Friday). Kinsus believes this was 
driven by financial management considerations, and not by any specific intention to reduce 
its stake in Kinsus. If Kinsus’ share price continues to rise, management does not rule out 
the possibility that its parent company may trim its stake again. Overall, we believe Kinsus’ 
fundamentals remain largely in line with our expectations, with raw material prices 
continuing to rise and substrate supply tightening gradually. We maintain our Buy (1) rating 
on the stock, with a 12-month target price of TWD1,068, based on a 36x PER on the 1-
year forward EPS. Key downside risks include weaker-than-expected demand for memory, 
PCs, gaming GPUs, and AI servers.

We hosted an online conference call for Kinsus after trading hours on 31 August. Overall, 
we believe the business outlook to be largely on track with our previous note (link here, 
published on 31 July). Below are our key takeaways from the conference.

Recent updates:  
• We believe the company has revised up its 3Q26 revenue growth target from 10% QoQ 
to 15% QoQ, reflecting a better-than-expected benefit from price increases. Looking 
ahead to 4Q26, management still expects growth of 10% QoQ, in line with its previous 
comments. 
• Revenue mix in 2Q26: ABF 45%, BT 38%, and contact lens 17%. Looking ahead, the 
company expects the revenue mix from ABF to gradually increase to 50–55% in 2027.

Price hike & margin expansion: 
• The company maintains its target of a 7-8% ASP increase per quarter in 2H26 to reflect 
rising costs of raw materials, including glass fibre, copper foil, drill bits, and ABF build-up 
film. 
• Although some competitors have highlighted a much larger magnitude of price hikes, 
Kinsus has not seen a similar level of increase. The company also believes the price 
hike level for ABF will be higher than that for BT in 2H26, whereas in 1H26 the price hike 
for BT was higher than that for ABF. 
• Looking into 2H26 and 2027, the company expects raw material ASPs to continue rising. 
As such, single-digit price hikes should be possible, with raw materials currently 
accounting for 30% of BT’s COGS. In addition, the driver of ASP increases is expected 
to gradually shift from raw material inflation to substrate capacity shortages.  
• In terms of gross margin expansion, Kinsus believes this will be driven mainly by an 
improved product mix and rising utilisation rates, rather than price hikes. This is similar 
to Unimicron. However, NYPCB’s gross margin expansion could be driven by internal 
price hikes within the Nanya Group.

 Key takeaways from investor conference call

### Page 2

Kinsus Interconnect Technology (3189 TT) 
31 August 2026 
2

Utilisation rates (UTR): 
• ABF: The company expects the UTR to increase from 85% (beginning of 2026) to 95% 
(at the end of 2026). 
• BT: The company expects the UTR to increase from 75% (beginning of 2026) to 85% (at 
the end of 2026).

Capacity expansion plan 
• Given capacity constraints, Kinsus will gradually expand its ABF capacity. It currently 
has around a 40 million ABF substrate capacity per month and plans to increase this to 
80 million by 2029. 
• According to management, the major equipment pull-in cycle will occur in 2027. After 
that, most equipment will be subsidised by end customers, and the depreciation 
expense should decline to TWD6.5-7.0bn from 2028 onward, versus TWD7.1-7.2bn in 
2026 and 2027. 
• As depreciation currently accounts for 15% of COGS, Kinsus believes it will reduce its 
selling prices slightly after clients begin subsidising the equipment, as the benefit will be 
shared between clients and Kinsus, at 7-8% each.

New growth opportunities – ABF 
• We believe Kinsus will secure a higher allocation for both CPU and GPU products from 
a global leading GPU brand in its next-generation products, given the current industry’s 
supply constraints. 
• The company also believes it will provide additional support to other GPU brands for 
their GPU products from 2027. 
• Outside of GPUs, the company sees less progress in the ASIC segment for now 
compared with GPUs. It has secured around a 10% allocation for one major ASIC 
product, but progress in other ASIC products remains relatively limited.

New growth opportunities – BT  
• The company believes pluggable optical modules and market share gains (as 
competitors gradually exit the BT market) will be key growth drivers for Kinsus. 
• According to management, the BT substrate used in optical modules will have 14 layers, 
compared with the mainstream 4-layer format today. Its existing equipment can support 
both 4-layer and 14-layer BT substrates. However, the company may need to add some 
routing capacity to support optical module demand.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

### Page 1

Flashnote
31 August 2026
MediaTek
NVDA and GOOG subscribe to MTK's US$3.9bn
ECB
What's new
• Today, MediaTek announced the largest ECB ever in the Taiwan market.
MediaTek announced today that it is issuing a US$3.9bn (>NT$120bn)
overseas convertible bond (ECB), the largest ECB ever from a Taiwan issuer
(see news here). NVIDIA subscribed US$3.5bn (~90%), which marks its
first major direct investment in a Taiwan-listed company. Alphabet also
subscribed (amount undisclosed). Per the announcement, the conversion
price is set at ~NT$4,500 (i.e. 115% of its 31 Aug close). Proceeds fund
foreign-currency material procurement, and the company disclosed a max
dilution of ~1.67%.
• NVIDIA and Alphabet subscribed. In a subsequent interview, NVIDIA and
MediaTek announced a deepened partnership across 1) AI infrastructure:
MediaTek adopts the NVLink Fusion platform, giving hyperscalers, CSPs
and frontier labs a prebuilt, pre-validated path to bring custom XPUs into
NVLink-connected rack-scale AI factories, with NVIDIA networking and CPUs
pulled through when MediaTek wins; 2) local AI computing: multi-generation
DGX Spark/RTX Spark PC roadmap; and 3) automotive: Dimensity Auto
with DRIVE AGX. MediaTek mgmt reiterated AI chip revenue of ~US$2bn
this year and its target of ~15% of an ~US$80bn TAM next year, which it
expects NVLink Fusion to support by shortening customers’ time to market.
Why it matters
• Endorsement of MediaTek's AI strategy. NVIDIA mgmt commented that
the investment formalises a partnership built on a decade-long roadmap,
and it is NVIDIA's first major investment in a Taiwan-listed company. We
believe a commitment of this scale is hard to repeat regularly, positioning
MediaTek as NVIDIA's partner of choice in the custom-XPU expansion,
marking an endorsement that peers are unlikely to obtain and confirmation
of MediaTek's expansion from AI ASIC chips into full rack-scale systems and
platforms.
• Dilution is small, and overhang risk is low. Disclosed max dilution is only
~1.67%. Moreover, >90% of the deal sits with strategic subscribers (i.e.
NVIDIA, Alphabet), who we believe will not run the delta-hedging (short-
selling) that typically pressure shares after an ECB issuance.
• Partnership enlarges MediaTek's ASIC opportunity. Through NVLink
Fusion, every MediaTek XPU rack pulls through NVIDIA switching, networking
& CPUs, so NVIDIA gains from MediaTek's ASIC success. We believe NVIDIA
chose MediaTek because it is open to both GPU-attached & ASIC business,
whereas peers compete with NVIDIA in networking or are less aligned. This
supports mgmt's target of ~15% of an US$80bn TAM next year.
What now
• Reiterate Outperform, TP at NT$10,000.
Semicons & Semicon Equip
Taiwan

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 5.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

Stock Data  
Price to Book Value 14.4x

### Page 3

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$5,230 is based on 23x 2H27-1H28 P/E. as we are constructive on its 
broadening ASIC pipeline to drive earnings upgrade cycle and re-rating as it locks in 
more supply.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating.  VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating)

INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a c ommon industry, 
sector, region or other classification(s). A stock’ s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from Credo's
FY1Q27 Results
We see positive implications for Bizlink from Credo's comments
on expanding AEC   and emerging ALC opportunities.
FY1Q27 (ended July 2026) results:
• Credo (CRDO.US, not covered) reported revenue of US$479mn (+10% QoQ/
+115% YoY), roughly in line with the consensus of US$474mn and guidance
of US$465-475mn.
• Non-GAAP GM was 68% (-0.3ppt QoQ) in line with consensus at 68% and

guidance at 67-69%.
• Non-GAAP NP was US$236mn (+4% QoQ/+140% YoY), roughly in line with
consensus at US$232mn.
• Four customers each contributed more than 10% of Credo's revenue in
FY1Q27, i.e., 33%, 28%, 13% and 10%, respectively.
FY2Q27 and FY27 outlook:
•  FY2Q27 revenue is guided to be US$525-535mn (+11% QoQ/+98% YoY at the
midpoint, largely in line with consensus of US$526mn), non-GAAP GM of 67-
69% (vs. consensus at 67.5%) and opex of US$100-105mn.

• For FY27, Credo now expects total revenue to grow >85% YoY (vs.
consensus at ~86% YoY), with an  inflection in 2H mainly from optical
revenue (>US$600mn for FY27 from Zero-Flap Optics, silicon photonics PICs
and optical DSPs, with each to contribute >US$100mn).
• Credo expects its FY27 GM to stay flat YoY, while operating expenses might
increase by ~55% YoY.
• 1.6T AEC might start to emerge from 2HFY27.
• ALCs and OmniConnect gearboxes remain on track for initial revenue from

FY28, management believes both are multi-billion dollar opportunities for
Credo.
• It also sees increasing customer activity on NPO for scale up network, with
confirmed PIC design wins for ramp in FY28.
Bizlink Read-across:
• We see positive implications from Credo's comments on the AEC's spec
upgrade and its penetration into more hyperscalers and neoclouds, as well as
the reiterated timing of ALC ramp in FY28.
• We stay constructive on Bizlink for its multiple drivers in the AI deployment

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): Jul rev in line at +54% YoY; AI server and smartphones to support 3Q 
growth

Hon Hai’s Jul revenues were +54% YoY/ +15% MoM to NT$947m, or 2% higher than our 
estimates, overall in line. From a MoM perspective, Consumer Electronics & 
Components delivered significant growth MoM, benefitting from the pull-in 
momentum, followed by Cloud and Networking & Computing which achieved strong 
growth MoM. From a YoY perspective, all segments across Consumer Electronics, 
Components, Cloud and Networking & Computing delivered strong growth YoY, and 
management attributed this to the strong pull-in momentum and higher selling prices of 
Consumer Electronics and Computing products. We expect 3Q26E revenues to be +48% 
YoY / +22% QoQ to NT$3.1 trillion. June/ July delivered strong YoY (+52%/ 54% YoY), 
and we expect Aug revenues to remain strong at 73% YoY / 11% MoM to NT$1,051bn, 
supported by AI rack server ramp-up, and strong seasonality of the consumer 
electronics business. Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI server in strong ramp up; PC to decline in 2H26 on high memory cost

sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on the memory price hike weighing on PC

generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up, which carries a higher ASP . Overall, 
we model Quanta’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 85% / 47% YoY, riding on the 
growing AI infrastructure trend. Maintain Neutral.

correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

AVC (3017.TW, Buy): Liquid cooling component ASP and GM upside on the rising complexity of server 
racks; July in line

AVC’s Jul revenue was up 57% YoY / 6% QoQ to NT$19bn, largely in line with our 
estimate. We attribute the strong YoY growth to the strong shipments of AI server liquid 
cooling components, riding on the increasing AI server end demand and a growing liquid 
cooling penetration rate. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
(1) rising shipments of next-generation rack-level AI servers, (2) an increase in liquid 
cooling dollar content in next-generation AI server racks given their growing complexity, 
and (3) a rising liquid cooling penetration rate in both ASIC and GPU AI servers. Maintain 
Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$3,986 on a target P/E multiple of 28.2x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): July and 2Q26 beat; AI server rack in a strong ramp-up with customer 
expansion and specification upgrades

Wistron’s July revenue was up 61% YoY to NT$308bn, or 30% higher than our estimate, 
reflecting strong AI server rack demand from neo-cloud and enterprises, offsetting the 
impact of the model transition. We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through 
Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26, which 
will drive up the ASP , along with strong AI spending and the expansion of company’s 
customer base. The new GPU AI server racks have various racks across GPU, CPU, and 
LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming quarters. On PC, the company’s July shipments 
were at 1.6m units (-20% MoM / YoY), reflecting modest demand under high memory 
costs. Despite PC shipments likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in 
since late 2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, 
driving 2H26 revenue up +28% HoH. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wistron 
Our 12-month target price of NT$281 is based on a 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived 
from PC/server peer EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a 
major factor for the stock’s share price performance.   
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected PC market recovery, 2) 
slower-than-expected AI server demand ramp-up, and 3) weaker-than-expected 
demand on general servers.

than our estimate, reflecting AI servers shipment ramp up. Jul PC shipments was down

the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, and PC product 
mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect Compal’s 3Q / 
4Q26E revenues to grow at 6% / 10% QoQ, benefiting from the rising AI server end 
demand from Neocloud clients. We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, 
supported by global capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, and Vietnam, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, which demand 
could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain Neutral on 
fair valuation.

vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM

Pegatron (4938.TW, Sell): AI server driving 2H26 growth; PC growth capped by rising memory cost; Sell

result and raise our 2026-28E net incomes by 10% / 8% / 7%, mainly on higher revenues 
and lower opex ratios. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 5% / 5% / 4%, mainly on 
higher contribution from AI server business. Our 2026-28E opex ratios are slightly 
revised up, mainly on higher R&D expense to support the AI server specification 
upgrades and PC product mix upgrade towards AI PC.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 1

•Broad-base 2Q, cost-capped margin. Mgmt. reiterated that 2Q26 growth 
was broad-based across every product line, end market and region, not a 
single AI pocket. GPM was slightly below Street, as raw material cost 
inflation offset part of the pricing and mix improvement. Internal 
manufacturing efficiency projects are underway to absorb it in the future.
•Jul price hike hits spot and direct. The Jul price adjustment covers spot 
prices and direct customers, vs. earlier rounds limited to contract 
customers, and spans c.50% of revenue including MLCC, tantalum and 
other capacitors. Spot pricing flows through immediately rather than at 
renewal, so mgmt. expects ASP and revenue effects shortly, with margin 
impact far more visible in 2H. Mgmt. also sees no sign of demand slowing 
down in the near-term.
•Tantalum now the largest line. AI is not an MLCC-only story for passive 
components. Tantalum grew fastest of any line in 2Q, suits AI power 
management, and has over taken magnetics to become Yageo's largest 
product line. MLCC also grew well and should keep growing in coming 
quarters. AI revenue is now 16% of total revenue, spread across tantalums, 
MLCCs, resistors and inductors rather than single product.
•65 sites, dual-region by product. Every product is manufactured in at least 
two regions, giving customers flexibility against tariff and geopolitical risks. 
Mgmt. believe the existing 65 facilities are flexible in adding new lines at a 
fast pace whenever shortages appear, saving customers 1-2 years vs. 
building from greenfield. The company also planned the capacity expansion 
ahead of demand.

Yageo (2327.TW)

•YFIII and overseas MLCC bases. YFIII in Kaohsiung, commissioned in 2024, is Yageo's largest MLCC fab and still has
space for additional MLCC lines, with AI-specific equipment arriving monthly since last year. Outside Taiwan, Yageo
has been opening new lines within existing facilities in Mexico and China, leaving it able to absorb incremental AI
orders without a new building cycle.
•Lead times extend, LTA interest rises. Lead time of most of the products has lengthened vs. 1H26. Tantalum is the

tightest, followed by MLCC and resistors. Mgmt. indicated all AI-related lines are extremely tight, and more
customers are now approaching Yageo to sign LTAs.
•NP0 leadership on the power side. Yageo estimates a meaningful share (at least 30-40%) of NP0 high-voltage
MLCC, based on the internal assessment. Built on EV and industrial experience, NP0 fits AI's high-voltage, high-
temp duty and sits on the DC power side, while high-cap MLCC serves the near-chip stage. Yageo ships direct to

leading power supply players and is invited as first batch of design partners.
•Low-to-high conversion only partial. Converting low-end MLCC capacity to high-end is only partially possible.
High-spec MLCC stores more capacitance through higher layer counts, requiring advanced stackers rather than
conventional equipment. Yageo can optimize and redeploy some existing tooling, but not fully substitute, which is
why it is committing capex to new AI production line instead of relying on conversion.

Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -8% MoM/+54%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Bizlink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales came in at NT$8,892mn (-8% MoM/+54% YoY)
• QTD reached 65% of our 3Q26 estimate at NT$28,521mn (+23% QoQ/+55%
YoY) and consensus at NT$27,268mn (+17% QoQ/+48% YoY).
Our view:
• We think 3Q26 revenue is tracking roughly in line with our estimate.
• Looking into 2H26, we expect HPC to remain the main growth driver, as

power interconnects should continue to see momentum from AI/general
servers. Meanwhile, data interconnects should grow along with new ASIC
model ramp and GPU sever demand.
• Moreover, semi cap equipment revenue growth should also sustain.
• Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Bizlink (3665.TW)
Base case, derived from 30x 2027e P/E. We think our target P/E multiple is justified by an
earnings CAGR of 46% seen over 2025-28e, which implies a PEG of around 0.7. This is in line
with the average of 0.7 in 2019-24, when the stock was trading at an average 13x P/E for a 19%
earnings CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Faster and wider AEC adoption
n Data center and semi equipment capex increases that drive stronger HPC and semi-cap

business
n Stronger recovery for automotive business
Risks to Downside
n Lower-than-expected AEC adoption
n Intensifying competition
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower-than-expected growth in the semi capital equipment market
n Continued weakness in the auto industry

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 3

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
07 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Asia Vital Components
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and the expansion of its addressable market.
Our TP of NT$3,800 is derived from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified given the
company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment, rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing

value of thermal content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural drivers still in place, we expect
the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a High Risk rating is not supported by other
qualitative factors such as cold-plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not been applied.
Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2)

heightened price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling
technologies.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 1

M
Update
Advantech  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -8% MoM/+88%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Advantech's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$10,419mn (-8% MoM/+88% YoY),
• QTD revenue reached 77% of MSe, at NT$28,185mn (+8% QoQ/+59% YoY),
and 76% of consensus, at NT$28,371mn (+9% QoQ/+60% YoY).
• By region, N.A (+106%) outperformed, while other markets, including Europe
(+52%), China (+67%), Taiwan (+96%), Japan (+73%), Korea (+79%)  and

emerging markets (+75%),  also performed strongly.
• By segment, Intelligent Systems was +93% YoY,   IoT Automation was +36%
YoY, while  Intelligent Service grew single-digit% YoY. The Embedded Sector
grew 61% YoY, of which Embedded IoT grew 83% YoY, while Applied
Computing grew 37% YoY.
Our view:
• We think its 3Q26 revenue is tracking ahead of market expectations and
more likely to approach 20-25% QoQ growth.
• Management attributed the strength in August to demand from sectors

including semi, AI datacenter, energy infrastructure, and factory automation.
• B/B ratio in August was stronger than that in July, continuing to point to a
strong revenue outlook in the coming quarters.
• We remain constructive on Advantech, as it is well positioned to benefit
from accelerating IoT market growth amid edge AI proliferation.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Advantech (2395.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by our 30% earnings CAGR
forecast for 2025-28. It implies a PEG of 1.0, vs. the stock's average one-year forward P/E of
25x in 2015-22 and the earnings CAGR of 8% during that period, with a PEG of 3.5.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected macroeconomic conditions
n More top- and bottom-line growth from the platform and applications

Risks to Downside
n Weaker macro environment
n Slower progress in edge AI implementation

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

公司簡介  
景碩科技股份有限公司創立於 2000 年 ， 為專
業載板全製程工廠 (Full Process Workshop) ，
提供客戶線路設計、光罩製作、基板生產到自
動電性檢測全製程服務 。其產品主要用途為半
導體構裝時作為晶片載體 ， 同時成為對外電路
連接的通道 ， 屬於封裝產業的原物料或者是載
體元件，銷售對象主要為國內外之 IC 封裝、
設計與系統業者， 主要生產基地位於台灣桃園
市新屋區、新竹縣新豐鄉、桃園市楊梅區及中
國江蘇省蘇州市。

2

營運展望 
1. 2Q26 需求強勁及 漲價效益 帶動營收獲利續成長 ：AI 需求帶動  ABF 與 BT 
進入結構型增長，以及漲價效益、需求強勁帶動第二季營收獲利大幅成長。景
碩第二季營收 125 億元、+12.5% QoQ、+30.7% YoY，毛利率 26.1%，營業利
益 17 億元、+88.5% QoQ、+170.8% YoY，營業利益率 13.4%。歸屬母公司稅
後淨利  13 億 元 ， 每 股 盈 餘 2.57 元 。 產 品 組 合 分 別 為 高 頻 環 氧 樹 脂 板 
(Ajinomoto Build -Up Fil m, ABF ) 45% (+27% YoY) 、 雙 馬 來 醯 亞 胺 樹 脂 板 
(Bismaleimide Triazine, BT) 38% (+39% YoY)、隱形眼鏡 (Lens) 17% (+24% YoY)。 
2. 高頻環氧樹脂板 (ABF) 稼動率維持高檔 ， 積極擴產滿足客戶需求：高速運算相
關 CPU、GPU、ASIC 等大晶片尺寸 應用仍然供不應求， 目前公司旗下三座  
ABF 工廠 (新屋廠、新豐廠、楊梅廠) 平均產能利用率約為 85%，隨 AI 需求
強勁增長及新專案陸續進場，預 期 2026 年底產能利用率將攀升至 90 ~ 95% 
之飽和區間。為因應未來幾年產能缺口，公司規劃於第六廠  (楊梅廠) 進行擴
產， 目前規劃分別為 2027 年擴增 25%、2028 年擴增 30%、2029 年擴增 23%。
儘管新產能開出時，因產能擴大將導致利用率短期下滑，但隨訂單持續挹注長
線利用率仍將朝 90 ~ 95% 之高檔水位靠攏。 
3. 雙馬來醯亞胺三嗪樹脂板 (BT) 受惠記憶體、光模組等強勁需求：因 AI 需求
強勁，記憶體與光模組 (Optical Module) 等客戶需求持續上修，目前 BT 平均
產能利用率約為 75%，預期 2026 年底產能利用率將攀升至 85%。既有產能已
能滿足記憶體和光模塊等客戶需求，故雙馬來醯亞胺樹脂板方面暫無擴產計畫。
傳統 電路板在進入 800G、1.6T 或 3.2T 世代後已無法滿足高頻細線路需求 ，
且以上規格生產過程 須導入載板及  mSAP 製程，業界 普遍共識光模組 已是 
2027 ~ 2029 年 BT 重要業務發展目標。 
4. 封裝載板全新上升循環，明後年新產能開出後營運獲利再上一階：受惠 AI 伺
服器與 HPC 需求，ABF 載板產能利用率近滿載並啟動擴產和漲價。同時 BT 
載板受惠記憶體與光模組市場強勁需求，也逐漸調漲報價反映成本。封裝載板
產業進入全新上升循環，預期下半年報價仍將逐季調漲。待明後年新產能陸續
開出後，營運將正式邁入爆發期 。合庫預估 2026 年營收 530 億元、+34.5% 
YoY，毛利率 26.4%。營業利益 71 億元、+167.2% YoY，營業利益率 13.5%。
歸屬母公司稅後淨利 53 億元，每股盈餘 10.25 元。 
5. ESG (景碩在 2026 年上市公司治理評鑑結果為 21 ~ 35%)：景碩除由永續經營
委員會提升跨部門協作以推動環境、社會與公司治理及氣候變遷因應之目標與
執行進度 ， 亦每季向董事會報告執行情形 ， 進一步強化內部組織治理精進作為 。
此外，公司亦強化供應鏈永續稽核作業，針對勞工權益、健康與安全及管理制
度等面向持續檢視與改善，致力建構永續合作網絡。

祥碩 (5269 TT) 
本業獲利不如預期

永豐觀點 
祥碩現金殖利率約 3%， 導入機器人供應鏈可視為題材支應 ， 股價可望較具有支撐 ，
只是 PC 市況欠佳仍難免影響營運動能，使得股價也不易顯著攀升，後續再觀察
PCIe Gen4、新款晶片組出貨態勢。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持中立：研究部上修今明年獲利，主要是業外收益貢獻顯著，本業獲利則因桌機
DIY 市況疲弱而不如預期。研究部預估祥碩 2026 年稅後 EPS 92.06 元(原預估
83.26 元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 95.17 元(原預估 88.95 元)。 考量桌機DIY 市況疲弱 ，
影響公司本業營運動能，維持中立建議。

ESG 評析 
祥碩企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

### Page 2

2

祥碩為高速傳輸晶片公司：祥碩產品線主要分為 PCIe、SATA 與 USB 三大類，祥
碩 2Q26 營收結構可區分為桌機晶片組 50%、host 晶片 20%、裝置端晶片 7%與
車載監控 22%。主控端晶片位於板卡端，產品包括PCIe to USB 或 PCIe to SATA、
PCIe Switch 晶片；裝置端為外接硬碟包括 USB to PCIe、USB to SATA 等晶片。
產品線毛利率以自有產品線的毛利率較高。 
2Q26 本業獲利不如預期：祥碩 2Q26 營收 27.23 億元(-22%QoQ，-20%YoY)，
毛利率 50.29%， 營業利益率23.84%， 稅後獲利20.17 億元(+9%QoQ，+80%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 27.19 元，本業獲利不如預期，係因營收滑落幅度大，而毛利率亦呈下滑
態勢。祥碩 2Q26 各項財務要點如下：(1) 營收季減 22%，除桌機 DIY 市場需求
疲軟、大客戶重心轉向伺服器而減少桌機 CPU 供貨外，大客戶所需晶片組的版本
變化也是因素之一。2025 年晶片組 AM4：AM5 比重=25：75，1Q26 AM5 比重
降至<70%、2Q26 AM5 比重更降至<50%，此因 AM5 搭載 DDR5，而 AM4 搭
載 DDR4，隨 DDR5 供給相當緊缺，故只能改以 AM4 供貨，而 AM4 單價又低於
AM5， 故進一步拖累營收下滑幅度。(2) 毛利率由 1Q26 的 50.81%降至 50.29%，
不如預期(原本期待隨晶片組比重滑落，毛利率應能上升)，主要也是 AM4 比重拉
升，尤其若往更低階版本出貨，對毛利率更不利。 (3) 業外收益認列 15.36 億元
(+36%QoQ)，主要來自權益法認列的關聯企業損益(文曄)挹注。 
3Q26 營收續降：祥碩 07/2026 營收 7.83 億元(-9.7%MoM，-38%YoY)，隨 CPU

### Page 3

3

缺貨與記憶體短缺，均影響桌機市場銷售， 公司預期今年桌機主板產業規模恐衰退
30%。公司預期 3Q26 營收季減約 10%，4Q26 隨 AM4 比重更增，營收亦應將續
降。針對毛利率走勢，公司目標值希冀仍維持>50%，影響毛利率的正負因子均存
在：正向因子為毛利率較低的晶片組比重下滑(目前預期 3Q26 晶片組維持 50%比
重、4Q26 比重下滑)，負向因子為製造成本上揚(尤其封測成本)，公司策略是希冀
自有品牌能全額轉嫁，但對大客戶(北美客戶)則維持原本價格，與客戶價格協商自
08/01 已開始進行並延續至 4Q26。研究部預估祥碩 3Q26 營收 24.48 億元(-
10%QoQ，-39%YoY)，毛利率 50.23%，稅後獲利 16.14 億元 (-20%QoQ，
+2%YoY)，稅後 EPS 21.75 元。整體而言，2026 年營運動能以自有品牌(比如中
國本土 PC 需求帶動 SATA/bridge controller/PCIe出貨攀升)、T echpoint預計年
成長約 5%、PCIe Gen4 2H26 開始出貨表現較佳。 
2027 年期待營收成長雙位數： 祥碩期待 2027 年營收成長雙位數幅度，主要由
AM5.5 2Q27 開始出貨、 ASIC 客戶出貨、 PCIe Gen4 明年起量、機器人相關
PCIe/USB4 host controller 挹注：(1) 大客戶下一代晶片組 AM5.5 預計於 2Q27
交貨 ， 以使客戶能於原定計畫3Q27 上市 。(2) 供應美系客戶 ASIC 晶片將於 2H26
開始少量出貨，主要用於 IPC 應用，第二顆 ASIC 晶片也已開案。接續尚有兩位
ASIC 客戶，不過時程較久，分別預計 2028、2029 年方步入量產。(3) PCIe Gen4 
七月已開始供貨 ， 目前以中國伺服器(比如背板擴充)與 IOT 先行 ，Gen5 預計 2026
年底~2027 年初 tape out(故出貨也須待至 2H27~2028 年初)、Gen6 則計畫 1H27
出測試晶片。(4) 導入中國與北美機器人供應鏈，祥碩供應 PCIe Gen3 與 USB4 
host controller。至於桌機市況，公司原預期 2H27 有機會回溫，但目前隨記憶體
與 CPU 供給吃緊，不排除 2027 整年度均持續受影響。再者，T echpoint則僅溫
和成長，主要是現階段出貨仍以類比高清產品為主 (終端市場以中國為主，近期導
入印度供應鏈)，若要往前裝市場放量則須待公司本身的 SerDes 介面準備妥善，
目前維持車載要較為顯著放量待 2028 年~2029 年。裝置晶片因 NAND Flash 昂
貴又短缺(大客戶將 NAND Flash 挪移至伺服器使用)，排擠外接硬碟使用(祥碩裝
置晶片主攻外接硬碟)。 
維持中立：研究部上修今明年獲利，主要是業外收益貢獻顯著，本業獲利則因桌機
DIY 市況疲弱而不如預期。研究部預估祥碩 2026 年營收 110.2 億元(-18%YoY)，
毛利率 50.39%， 稅後獲利68.3 億元(+26%YoY)， 稅後EPS 92.06 元(原預估 83.26
元)，並預估 2027 年營收 120.96 億元(+10%YoY)，毛利率 50.84%，稅後獲利
70.62 億元(+3%YoY)，稅後 EPS 95.17 元(原預估 88.95 元)。考量桌機 DIY 市況
疲弱，影響公司本業營運動能，維持中立建議。

藥華藥 8/29/2026 公 告，旗 下 新 藥 Ropeg 正式獲得美國 FDA 核准使用
於 原 發 性 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 藥證 ：  
藥華藥 8/29/2026 公告 ， 旗 下 新 藥 Ropeg 正 式 獲 得 美 國 FDA 核 准 使 用 於 原
發 性 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET )藥 證， 預計 全 美 約 17 萬名 ET 患 者，有 望 跨入 逾 400
億 美 元 市 場 商 機。  
此次 FDA 核 准 即 ET 全 線 用 藥 ， 全 面 涵 蓋 所 有 病 患 ，不 區 分 基 因 型 或 疾 病
狀態， 也 包括新診斷 、 尚未接受細胞減量治療患者 ，是 首款專門用於治療
ET 的 干 擾 素 療 法。  
公司表示 在市場准入、行銷及銷售方面 已有豐富經驗 ，供應鏈及上市準備
已到位 ， 治 療 版 圖 由 PV 拓 展 至 ET ， 可以 讓美國 ET 病 患 盡 早 受 惠。  
訴訟方面， 目前 AOP 仍為 藥 華 藥 的 Ropeg 歐 洲 授 權 商 ， 雙 方 具 備 共 同 商 業
利益， 不 排 除 最 終 走 向 和 解 或 重 新 協 議 供 貨 條 件 的 可 能 性。  
注射筆 6 月 取 得 美 國 FDA 核准， 7 月 正 式 在 美 國 上 市 ， 提 升 患 者 用 藥 便 利
性 ， 提 高 新 病 患 採 用 率 及 持 續 用 藥 意 願。 Ropeg 治療 ET (原 發 性 血 小 板 過 多
症 )已 經 取 證 ， 公 司 已 提 前 展 開 商 業 化 部 署 ， 藥 證 獲 准 後將 快 速 上 市 銷售 。

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 8 月 31 日

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為 四 個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」 或「區間操作」 之投資評
等，就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基
本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

1 
6944 兆聯實業(Mega Union)

報告日期： 2026/09/03  研 究 員：翁浩軒 (Davy Wong) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 半導體擴廠帶動公司在手訂單創高  
受惠於 AI 帶動半導體需求成長，加上全球供應鏈分散策略，半導
體廠積極擴產，隨客戶新建廠案增加，帶動公司在手訂單持續創
高 ， 截至 6 月底 ， 兆聯實業在手訂單 277 億 ， 高於 2025 年底之 212.6
億，而 7 月公告承攬美國高科技產業客戶之廢水處理系統工程 50
億元以上，因公司海外工程效率提升，工期在 2 年內可完成，因
此目前超過 300 億之在手訂單將使公司今明年營收持續成長。

二.  2026 年營收雙位數成長，預估全年 EPS 40.25 元 
展望 2026 下半年，隨著接單逐步轉化為營收，下半年營收逐季走
揚，以工程認列為主，服務類產品在去年下半年導入新材料基期
較高下，營收年增率放緩。大客戶先進製程供不應求，近年於國
內外續擴建新廠，公司接單能見度預期可達 2030 年，此外記憶體
客戶於國內外新建廠案亦將持續貢獻，因美系記憶體客戶毛利率
較好，預期下半年毛利率維持高檔水準。預估 2026 年營收 213.01
億(YoY+25.95%)，稅後淨利 37.8 億(YoY+59.41%)，EPS 40.25 元。

三.  2027 年營收獲利將創新高，預估 EPS 47.52 元 
展望 2027 年，受惠於歷史新高的在手訂單，國內外半導體工程專
案大規模專案陸續認列將帶動營收獲利持續創高，半導體大客戶
先進製程於台灣及美國工程、國內外記憶體客戶之工程將是主要
貢獻來源，搭配服務類收入 2027 年亦將維持雙位數成長，毛利率
方面，海外專案執行效率穩健，唯半導體大客戶議價能力高，加
上美元強升不易，保守預估毛利率較 2026 年微幅下滑。預估兆聯
實業 2 0 2 7 年營收 2 6 5 . 0 億( Y o Y + 2 4 . 4 1 % )，稅後淨利 4 7 . 3 7 億
(YoY+25.31%)，EPS 47.52 元。

四. 投資建議：買進，目標價 950 元  
兆聯憑藉深耕半導體純水/廢水系統，與國內外半導體客戶長期配
合，具技術經驗與實績，隨著全球半導體產潮，兆聯訂單能見度
提高，營收亦將持續成長。考量到 1)公司在手訂單創高將帶動後
續營收創高、2)海外施工效率提升有助毛利率表現、3) 評價已落
至歷史區間中下緣，參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 20 倍本益比，目標價
950 元，建議買進。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
兆聯實業成立於 2004 年，是一家高科技廠房水務系統工程服務廠
商，從事純水及廢水回收系統規劃設計之工程，主要核心業務為
純水系統工程、廢水處理與回收水系統工程、系統營運與維護服
務、客製化耗材、樹脂再生、RO 膜清洗及海水淡化工程。公司以
專案方式承攬業主高科技產業純水、廢水處理系統、廢水回收系
統設備之規劃及設計工程，與業主簽訂工程統包合約，並依專案
需求進行工程發包、設備採購等作業，近年也提供成廠區水務系
統維運及耗材清洗與銷售服務，給予客戶在地化之系統設計、施
工 、 維運之一條龍整合服務 。 2025 年營收比重 ： 工程收入約占 79% 、
其他營業收入占 21%。2025 年產品之銷售地區比重：台灣約占
69%、美國占 19%、新加坡占 10%、大陸佔 2%。公司客戶以半導
體產業廠商為主，亦涵蓋 PCB、面板、LED、太陽能等廠商。國內
外競爭對手包含中鼎、萬年清、聯純等。

二.2026 年營運概況及 2027 年展望 
1. 半導體擴廠帶動公司在手訂單創高 
兆聯實業核心業務為半導體、記憶體、先進封裝與載板廠之超純
水系統、廢水回收系統規劃設計、設備整合、工程建置，並延伸
至長期代操維運、耗材與化學品銷售，受惠於 AI 帶動半導體元件
需求大幅成長，加上全球供應鏈分散策略，半導體廠積極擴產，
隨客戶新建廠案增加，帶動公司在手訂單持續創高，截至今年 6
月底，兆聯實業在手訂單 277 億，高於 2025 年底之 212.6 億，而 7
月公告承攬美國高科技產業客戶之廢水處理系統工程 50 億元以

2. 2Q26 毛利率優於預期，EPS 8.75 元 
2Q26 營收 50.80 億元(QoQ+7.94%、YoY+36.14%)，其中工程營收占
比 79.4%，營收較去年同期成長 48%，主要因海內外建廠案貢獻，
服務營收占比 20.6%，營收亦較去年同期成長 32%，毛利率升至
28.44%創單季新高，主要因記憶體客戶為加快建廠進度而有較高
利潤，加上海外專案工期縮短，有效提升工程效率，此外美金升
值亦有所助益，稅後淨利 8.72 億(QoQ+12.82%、YoY+70.76%)，EPS 
8.75 元。

3. 2026 年營收雙位數成長，預估全年 EPS 40.25 元 
展望 2026 下半年，隨著接單工程逐步轉化為營收，下半年營收逐
季走揚，以工程認列為主，服務類產品在去年下半年導入新材料
基期較高下，營收年增率放緩。大客戶先進製程供不應求，近年
於國內外續擴建新廠，國內部分，大客戶於新竹、台中、高雄持
續建置 2nm 及 2nm 以下之先進製程 ， 美國P2 目前施作中將於今年
認列大多數營收，隨著大客戶於美國建置大規模先進製程聚落及
先進封裝產能，公司接單能見度預期可達 2030 年，此外記憶體客
戶於國內外新建廠案亦將持續貢獻，且因美系記憶體客戶毛利率
較好，預期下半年公司毛利率仍維持 28% 左右之高檔水準。預估
兆聯實業 2026 年營收 213.01 億(YoY+25.95%)，毛利率 27.97%，稅
後淨利 37.8 億(YoY+59.41%)，EPS 40.25 元。

4. 2027 年營收獲利將創新高，預估 EPS 47.52 元 
展望 2027 年，受惠於歷史新高的在手訂單，國內外半導體工程專
案大規模專案陸續認列將帶動營收獲利持續創高，半導體大客戶
先進製程於台灣及美國工程、國內外記憶體客戶之工程將是主要
貢獻來源，搭配服務類收入 2027 年亦將維持雙位數成長，毛利率
方面，海外專案執行效率穩健，唯半導體大客戶議價能力高，加
上美元強升不易，保守預估毛利率較 2026 年微幅下滑。預估兆聯
實業 2027 年營收 265.0 億(YoY+24.41%)，毛利率 27.75%，稅後淨
利 47.37 億(YoY+25.31%)，EPS 47.52 元。

2026/09/03 投資速報

汽車零組件 
朋程 (8255.TT) 
2H26 營運仍平淡，等待工業事業帶來實質貢獻

中立(維持評等) 
出刊緣由：更新近況

個股股價報酬率 7.7 -17.4 15.6 11.3

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

研究部對朋程營運看法維持中性，主要是考量：(1)2H26 受制於銅
價上漲，以及上游晶圓代工費用調漲，毛利率空間受限 ；(2)48V 
MOSFET 模組客人拉貨需求減少，第三條產線未滿載，影響營收動
能；(3)新業務 SiC 項目尚在送樣驗證階段，需至明年才能有較大
貢獻。整體而言，考量朋程 1H26 業績表現不佳，加上2H26 毛利率
仍受壓，費用率升高等負面因素，研究部下修朋程今年獲利 17%，
預估 2026 年 EPS 6.01 元(-4.9%YOY)。

展望2027 年 ， 因來自於高單價ULLD 產品接單動能續強，48V MOSFET
模組第三條產線稼動有望改善，以及高毛利客製化 IGBT 工業品量
產，並帶來實質貢獻，預期朋程明年業績有望回升，估計 2027 年
EPS 6.77 元(+12.8%YOY)。評價上，以明年獲利為評價基礎，給予
23 倍本益比，目標價 155 元，潛在漲幅空有限，維持中立評等。

重點摘要：

1. 2Q26營業利益YOY下滑，稅後淨利因業外低基期而呈現年增： 
朋程2Q26合併營收下滑至20.32億元(-0.3% QOQ，-6.1% YOY)，
主因是歐洲客人拉貨放緩，48V MOSFET模組出貨減少所致。
此外 ， 公司有四成產品線需承受銅價上漲壓力 ，限制毛利率表
現，2Q26毛利率27.0%(季增0.1個百分點 ， 年減0.5個百分點)；
因研發費用仍高，2Q26費用率20.6%(季持平，年增3.9個百分
點；2Q26營業利益1.30億元(-0.3% QOQ，-44.5%YOY)， 本業獲
利能力受成本費用影響而較去年同期顯著衰退。

在最終獲利方面，朋程2Q26稅後淨利1.46億元(-15.4%QOQ，
+276%YOY)，2Q26EPS 1.44元。淨利呈現季減年增，主因為：
(1)因匯兌收益較少，以致業外收益自1Q26的0.40億元縮減至
0.28億元，故獲利環比呈現下滑；(2)同比大增則源於去年同
期認列約2億元匯損影響，以致基期極低所致。

2. 2H26營收逐季增溫，但毛利率有壓，最終獲利預期呈現年減： 
展望下半年 ，受惠超效能二極體(ULLD)訂單滿載 ， 加上工業事
業部之客製化IGBT產品將於4Q26開始小量量產， 預期營收將
逐季微幅升溫，預估 朋程3Q26營收21.32億元(+5.0% QOQ ，
+7.3% YOY) ， 預估 4Q26 營收 22.56 億元 (+5.8%QOQ ，
+22.7%YOY)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

不過，銅價高檔的負面影響仍在，加上上游晶圓代工費用調
漲，令毛利率改善空間 受限，管理層預期 2H26毛利率約在
26~28%的水準。研究部預期朋程2H26本業獲利將較1H26下滑；
相較去年同期 ，則會有顯著回升， 主要是因去年受關稅影響顯
著，2H25基期特低所致。

業外方面 ， 因去年下半年有較高的匯兌收益貢獻 ， 墊高業外基
期 ， 今年下半年業外收益則預期將較去年同期縮減 ，故令朋程
2H26稅後淨利 預期 較去年同期呈現衰退 。研究部預估朋程
3Q26EPS 1.49 元(-23.6%YOY) ； 預估 4Q26 EPS 1.3 8 元(-
17.5%YOY)。

3. 今年獲利下修，預估2026年EPS 6.01元： 
全年度來看 ，考量朋程1H26業績表現不佳 ， 加上2H26毛利率仍
受壓 ， 費用率持續升高等負面因素 ， 研究部下修朋程今年獲利
17%。研究部預估朋程2026年合併營收84.57億元(+4.3%YOY)，
毛利率26.8%(持平)，費用率20.8%(+1.5pct)，稅後淨利下滑
至6.11億元(-4.9%YOY)，預估全年EPS 6.01元。

表一、朋程 2026 年財報預估調整

4. 48V MOSFET新產能未滿載，採用自製晶片進度延後： 
48V MOSFET模組的部分 ，目前第三條產線已完成建置與驗證 ，
但2Q26受歐洲客人拉貨需求疲軟影響 ， 產能利用率尚未滿載 ，
年出貨量維持約300萬顆。客人將於今年10月給予最新展望，
預期2027年隨PHEV與HEV需求提升，銷量預估有5~10%成長空
間，有望填滿產線 。在自研晶片部分 ， 因為上游晶圓代工報價
調漲 ， 為確保合約毛利率 ， 公司策略性地延後半年至一年再導
入 ， 短期仍維持採用歐系晶片 ， 因此先前期待該產品毛利率提
高的利多暫時落空。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

5. 成立工業產品新事業部，積極開發IGBT/SiC： 
朋程的工業產品部門成功開發客製化IGBT產品並通過認證，
鎖定數位相機與工控領域 （包含6家相機客戶 ， 年需求約1千萬
顆） 。管理層表示，4Q26起單月出貨量將達150K，開始有營收
貢獻；並預計1Q27起單季出貨量將從500K逐季成長至800K。由
於客製化IGBT毛利率高於車用平均，將成為2027年提升獲利
結構的核心產品之一。

在SiC產品方面，朋程佈局650V/1200V/1700V三大SiC功率模
組平台，且集團已具備一條龍整合優勢，2H26以樣品認證為
主，希望在2027年邁入量產期。 未來產品目標市場分別為：
(1)SiC IPM ：瞄準日本家用空調環保法規升級需求，主推
10A~50A產品，預計2027年進入爆發期。(2)HVDC與AI伺服器：
聚焦數據中心的HVDC高壓傳輸專案 ， 最快4Q26提供樣品認證。

6. 明年營運有望改善，預估2027年EPS 6.77元： 
展望2027年 ， 考量歐美對燃油車需求增加 ，對高效能引擎需求
強勁 ， 令朋程ULLD繼續有偏強的增速。 目前ULLD訂單已滿載至
明年，且因單價高出傳統二極體6倍，能支持整體二極體事業
營收穩定成長。此外，48V MOSFET模組第三條產線稼動有望
改善，以及高毛利客製化IGBT工業品量產，並帶來實質貢獻，
預期朋程明年業績有望回升 。研究部預估朋程2027全年營收
達92.92億元(+9.9%YOY)，毛利率回升 至27.7%，費用率降至
19.7% ， 預 估 明 年 稅 後 淨 利6.90 億元 (+12.8%YOY) ，預估
2027EPS 6.77元。

Document 158: A Primer on AI Lab & AI Hyperscaler Unit Economics

### Page 1

U.S. Internet
A Primer on AI Lab & AI
Hyperscaler Unit Economics
We expect some confusion as AI labs start reporting GAAP
financials, as business mix and revenue recognition can vary.
Similar to how UBER and LYFT are in the same core business
yet gross bookings and net revenue have different definitions,
comparing AI labs may prove difficult.
The Key Take-Away: The unit economics of AI labs are similar across like-for-like product lines,
but business mix, revenue recognition, and partnerships can lead to fairly significant differences

in how financials are presented. This report attempts to break down these nuances and how
revenue and costs ultimately flow through the P&L at the AI lab. We also look at how the unit
economics of AI labs flow into the unit economics of the supporting hyperscalers.
Business Mix Differences Explain a Lot
AI labs have revenue lines across a number of different products and services. API was the
original AI lab business model, but in recent years applications and subscription products have

entered the mix. Shown below in Figure 1, hypothetical frontier lab "A" has upwards of 70% of
revenue coming from API and 30% from subscription. Hypothetical frontier lab "B" has the
inverse, closer to 80% of revenue from subscription. As we will illustrate further down in this
report, API tends to have higher inference margin than subscription, which in addition to
training costs being completely different, explains most of the difference in gross margins (after

training) at the aggregate AI lab level. It's possible that some of the training costs may be
recorded in R&D for various AI labs, so for the purposes of this report, we are showing ~10% of
training costs in COGS, which represents the final training run for an AI model. It's unclear at this
point how training costs are likely to be allocated across P&L lines by AI labs.
As we will discuss below, other major factors explaining revenue and margin differences are: 1)

AI lab unit economics have improved greatly in '26 (vs. '25) driven by agentic workflows and
enterprise becoming the "must buy" products in the market today. API and enterprise accounts
likely carry higher inference margins; hence, as mix shifts in that direction, and as AI models are
more token efficient per task, inference margins have surged higher. Based on our estimates
shown in Figure 2, we think frontier labs "A" and "B" may have increased paid inference margins
to upwards of 50%-65%+ in '26 (up from mid-teens % in '25), and possibly much higher on
select products and services. After factoring in final training costs (10% of overall training costs),
labs have seen the adjusted gross margin increase ~30-50 points vs. '25. AI lab "B" has strategic
partner revenue sharing which impacts profitability until certain thresholds are achieved, likely
eliminated to zero sometime after '28.

Based on the mix noted above and some of the revenue sharing agreements, training costs, and
inference costs, we conclude that around $35-$40 of every $100 in AI Lab revenue flows to
hyperscaler revenue in '26, on which the hyperscalers generate nearly $10-$20 in OI (at high
~35%-45% operating margin). Partner revenue share results in higher hyperscaler OI margin,
but the actual profit on a per token basis is likely the same excluding these fees. We think there
is a decent possibility that AI lab margins and hyperscalers' OI margins are higher than what we
illustrate here currently in '26, but we would assume these margins come down a bit over time
as competition increases at the frontier and as compute scarcity eases.

AI Products Have a Wide Range of Unit Economics,
and Are Often Recorded Differently
Noted above, the product mix at frontier labs can be dramatically different. Figures 4-6 below
illustrate that even within the same products (like for like) there can be nuances that drive

different unit economics including partnerships (revenue shares) and how actual revenues are
recorded (gross vs. net). Like UBER/LYFT , despite being the same core business, the revenue and
margin can be completely different.
Subscription products (like Claude Code or Codex) generally have lower inference margins than
API as some users benefit from AI labs' willingness to subsidize token cost to remain
competitive and retain users. Subscription products are both monthly-fee based and usage

based, often with caps which can be reset from time to time (we have seen an increase in cap
resets of late, likely in response to churn reduction efforts and model efficiency gains).
Subscription products tend to default to a specific model, but the user can toggle between
models and latency modes for various jobs, which can burn through token credits faster or
slower depending on model size. Enterprise subscriptions are often dollar-based by license

(often at higher limits than Plus and Pro plans), with daily caps that can be allocated across the
licenses. We estimate inference margins on subscriptions are in the 70% range. Noted above, we
would guess that the '26 inference margin may be higher than this level, but over time the
industry may drift back down towards this level to the degree that competitive alternatives
show up and if/when compute moves into surplus (vs. today's shortages).
Hyperscalers generate solid unit economics on agentic subscription products as the compute

being provisioned for these stateful runtime products often involves some "up the stack"
software products like databases, etc., and in some cases have revenue sharing between the AI
lab and the hyperscalers.

Direct API is the original business model for most frontier labs (before even products like
ChatGPT were introduced) and carries very attractive unit economics for both the AI lab and the
hyperscaler. Direct API is where a developer like Cursor or Figma embeds features inside their
products that call on the AI lab's API to generate tokens. The business model for the AI lab is
usage based on token consumption (i.e., pay-as-you-go). Inference margins for API have been
trending up as the models have improved token efficiency (i.e., the volume of tokens to
complete a task) while headline API token prices have increased. Inference providers have
similarly improved the efficiency of serving models at the infrastructure layer (via quantization,
speculators, and next-gen compute), supporting higher hyperscaler margins. Lastly, revenue
sharing between the AI lab and the hyperscaler further boosts the latter's margins (at the
expense of the former).
We have heard that AI lab inference margins on API are running well north of the levels noted in
Fig 5 in 2Q26, but we would guess this may normalize lower at some point (but it's not entirely
clear when).

The Indirect API business model is more or less the same as Direct in terms of what the end user
sees, but billing and the go-to-market relationship is between the user and the hyperscaler
(without direct engagement with the AI lab). AI lab "A" records indirect API revenue on a gross
basis, and it is becoming a larger part of the story. AI lab "B" doesn't have significant revenue
here and records revenue on a net basis, and may exclude revenue recognition entirely when its
strategic partner runs indirect API.
So similar to UBER and LYFT , as indirect grows as a percent of the overall AI lab story, it likely
creates distortion between reported revenues, and hence can distort the narrative around
relative AI lab progress.

Putting It All Together
Right now, just about every dollar of AI lab ARR is finding its way to the hyperscaler revenue line,
because of the high mix of training relative to inference in '26. Over time, inference profits
should eventually surpass training costs, and drive higher profit margins for the AI labs.
Hyperscalers likely start to lose market share of AI lab training and inference starting in '28
when backstopped AI infrastructure projects come online and become the first option for AI
labs. We generally see Amazon Web Services (AWS), Azure (MSFT , covered by Raimo Lenschow),
and Google Cloud Platform (GCP) controlling a similar mix of AI lab compute spend in the next
two years, before this shift to backstopped infrastructure kicks in.

Guide to the Barclays Fundamental Equity Research Rating System:
Our coverage analysts use a relative rating system in which they rate stocks as Overweight, Equal Weight or Underweight (see definitions below)
relative to other companies covered by the analyst or a team of analysts that are deemed to be in the same industry (the "industry coverage universe").

In addition to the stock rating, we provide industry views which rate the outlook for the industry coverage universe as Positive, Neutral or Negative (see
definitions below). A rating system using terms such as buy, hold and sell is not the equivalent of our rating system. Investors should carefully read the
entire research report including the definitions of all ratings and not infer its contents from ratings alone.
Stock Rating
Overweight - The stock is expected to outperform the unweighted expected total return of the industry coverage universe over a 12-month investment

horizon.
Equal Weight - The stock is expected to perform in line with the unweighted expected total return of the industry coverage universe over a 12-month
investment horizon.
Underweight - The stock is expected to underperform the unweighted expected total return of the industry coverage universe over a 12-month
investment horizon.
Rating Suspended - The rating and target price have been suspended temporarily due to market events that made coverage impracticable or to

comply with applicable regulations and/or firm policies in certain circumstances including where the Investment Bank of Barclays Bank PLC is acting in
an advisory capacity in a merger or strategic transaction involving the company.
Industry View
Positive - industry coverage universe fundamentals/valuations are improving.
Neutral - industry coverage universe fundamentals/valuations are steady, neither improving nor deteriorating.
Negative - industry coverage universe fundamentals/valuations are deteriorating.

Valuation Methodology: We value shares of AMZN using a sum-of-the-parts methodology for its retail and AWS businesses. Our valuation of AMZN's

retail business is based on a 20x EBITDA multiple using a blend of our FY27/28 estimates; while we value AWS using a 6x multiple on a blend of our
FY27/28 revenue estimates. Our resulting price target is $365.
Risks which May Impede the Achievement of the Barclays Research Valuation and Price Target: Downside risks in the short term that could
negatively impact our model and consensus estimates include 1) greater-than-expected slowdown in AWS against the weak macro backdrop, and

hence lower-than-expected OI margins, over a more drawn-out period; 2) further erosion in consumer spending and a persistent input/wage cost
inflation could pressure the Retail business. Longer-term risks include the highly competitive ecommerce landscape, increasing shipping costs from
same and next-day delivery, which could be a drag on margins, security breaches or frequent downtime in the AWS business, which could slow down
public cloud adoption, or execution missteps that could slow growth of the retail business.

Sustainable Investing Related Research: There is currently no globally accepted framework or definition (legal, regulatory or otherwise) of, nor

market consensus as to what constitutes a ‘sustainable’ , ‘ESG’ , ‘green’ , ‘climate-friendly’ or an equivalent company, investment, strategy or
consideration or what precise attributes are required to be eligible to be categorised by such terms. This means there are different ways to evaluate a
company or an investment and so different values may be placed on certain sustainability credentials as well as adverse ESG-related impacts of
companies and ESG controversies. The evolving nature of sustainable investing considerations, models and methodologies means it can be

challenging to definitively and universally classify a company or investment under a sustainable investing label and there may be areas where such
companies and investments could improve or where adverse ESG-related impacts or ESG controversies exist. The evolving nature of sustainable
finance related regulations and the development of jurisdiction-specific regulatory criteria also means that there is likely to be a degree of divergence
as to the interpretation of such terms in the market. We expect industry guidance, market practice, and regulations in this field to continue to evolve.
